Problems & improvements

问题与改进

每条按等级与类型标注，展开可看触发条件、实际观察、验收标准、证据原文与可直接粘贴的上游 Issue 草稿。评审发现不等于本轮实际阻塞，也不因业务通过而标为已解决。

独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39165536643 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 1

轻微易用性改进上游未复核反馈复核：证据支持原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · 非法用户名的错误没有指出允许的字符集

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

核心结论成立：公开 Skill 给出用户名语法（E14），但 user administration 的 create 只做 trim/小写并把 username 直接交给 Better Auth（E15/E81），实际失败只留下泛化的 Username is invalid（E16）；应用最终把 it-demo-service 改成 itdemo_service 绕行（E76/E77）。需要修正的是错误可定位性，不是宣称框架违反既有错误码承诺。E16 中另一条 Kysely countAll 失败是独立测试代码问题，不能据此说三组失败全由用户名造成；但这不影响本条关于错误信息缺口的主张。

  • 职责与公开约定：E14 明确用户名只允许 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，并要求后续账号变更走 user administration；E73 把该服务的公开错误限定为冲突/密码等六类并建议映射稳定 HTTP 响应，E78 与 E80 也显示 create/update 签名和 400 文档没有用户名格式错误。故字符集/长度是公开契约，缺少对应的可定位错误是改进空间，而不是把愿望写成违约。 E14 E73 E78 E80
  • 实际行为证据：E15/E81 显示 create 仅 trim+lowercase，随后直接 internalAdapter.createUser，catch 只处理唯一约束，其他错误原样抛出；E74 显示认证层使用 Better Auth 默认 username 插件。E16 的实际样例数据运行只输出 `Username is invalid`/`APIError: Username is invalid`；E76 显示旧 fixture 用 it-demo-service 时 Agent 只能推断连字符并改名。应用侧现有路由 schema 也只校验非空（E79），没有前置字符集/长度说明。 E15 E81 E74 E16 E76 E79
  • 应用接入与业务边界：E5 显示应用样例数据确实调用 userManagement.create 并传入 person.username；E76 记录旧 fixture 使用 it-demo-service 等连字符账号，违反 E14 的语法并触发 E16；E77 显示最终改为 itdemo_service 等下划线账号。应用选错用户名是触发条件，但这正是错误信息应帮助定位的场景，改名绕行只修了应用数据，未补框架错误反馈。 E5 E76 E14 E16 E77
  • 环境因素：失败路径是本地 @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication 的 Better Auth username 校验（E15/E74），不是外部服务、凭据或安装缺失；E16 是本地测试运行的原始输出。按 review-input 的 basis，当前冻结版本为 app-plugin-authentication 2.0.0-beta.4，错误文本来自该路径，不能用最新上游替代。没有证据显示环境把详细错误吞掉；公开 schema/文档本身就没有该信息（E79/E80）。 E15 E74 E16 E79 E80
  • 工厂因素：错误发生在包内创建路径（E15/E81）和默认 Better Auth 插件（E74），不是工厂注入配置、QA 或构建脚本；工厂提供的认证 Skill 还明确写出了用户名语法（E14），因此也不是提示缺失。未纳入仓库外 control 脚本，无法排查其他工厂问题；但本条候选没有工厂归因证据。 E15 E81 E74 E14
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：公开的 users 创建入口 CreateUserInput 对 username 只要求非空（E79），OpenAPI 400 只列密码/角色错误（E80），UserAdministrationService 的公开错误码也不含用户名格式（E78），实现仅 trim/lowercase（E15）。因此没有现成公开入口能返回允许字符集/长度；需要新增前置校验或把 Better Auth 错误映射为带这些信息的稳定错误。 E79 E80 E78 E15

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

认证 Skill 明确用户名只能是 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，但 userAdministration.create 的 optionalUsername 只做 trim 和小写化，随后由 Better Auth 抛出不带约束说明的 APIError: Username is invalid。历史修复轮使用带连字符的 it-demo-service 等账号，样例数据测试因此整组失败，最终只能在应用侧改名绕行。

实际影响
调用方无法从错误判断是长度、字符集还是重名；Agent 需要阅读认证插件内部实现或反复试错，增加一轮修复成本，且同样的错误在其他应用中会重复出现。
建议改法
在 user-administration 的创建/更新路径按文档语法做前置校验，返回结构化 400（例如 USERNAME_INVALID，并说明允许的字符与长度）；或在捕获 Better Auth 的 username 校验错误时映射为同样的稳定错误。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
通过 userManagement.create 创建 username 为 it-demo-service 的账号（含连字符，不符合 Skill 的 3–30 位字母/数字/下划线/点规则）。
应有能力 / 行为
创建接口返回可定位的 400，说明用户名允许的字符集和长度；或创建前即拒绝并给出同一说明。
实际观察
user-administration 仅 trim/lowercase 后交给 Better Auth，最终日志只得到 APIError: Username is invalid，没有字段、字符集或长度信息。
应用绕行与代价
应用把演示账号改为 itdemo_service 等下划线形式，并同步调整测试和登录；框架侧未修复。
改进后的验收标准
新增用例：创建含连字符或超长用户名时返回 400 且 message/字段错误码明确指出允许的 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点；合法用户名仍创建成功。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:143–143E14

Skill 明确用户名只允许 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，并说明初始化账号与后续账号都应走用户管理服务。

Before the first seed run, set `users.initialAdmin.username`, `users.initialAdmin.email` and `users.initialAdmin.password` in application configuration to customize the bootstrap account. Omitting the entire node preserves `nocobase` / `admin@nocobase.com` / `admin123`; an explicit node requires a nonempty password and defaults the username to `nocobase` and the email to `admin@nocobase.com`. Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots, emails must be valid addresses, and both are stored lowercase. Initialization only runs when the user table is empty, hashes the password and grants the newly created account root permission through the authorization plugin. After initialization, configuration changes do not reset accounts or rerun the recorded seeds. The root permission seed selects the administrator by the same configured username. Use the user administration service for subsequent account changes. Do not edit historical seeds or add a competing administrator seed.
packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:343–346E15

optionalUsername 只做 trim 和小写化，没有按文档语法校验长度或字符集，随后直接把 username 交给 createUser。

function optionalUsername(value) {
    const normalized = value?.trim().toLowerCase();
    return normalized || undefined;
}

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [轻微][易用性改进] 非法用户名的错误没有指出允许的字符集

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.4）、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

核心结论成立：公开 Skill 给出用户名语法（E14），但 user administration 的 create 只做 trim/小写并把 username 直接交给 Better Auth（E15/E81），实际失败只留下泛化的 Username is invalid（E16）；应用最终把 it-demo-service 改成 itdemo_service 绕行（E76/E77）。需要修正的是错误可定位性，不是宣称框架违反既有错误码承诺。E16 中另一条 Kysely countAll 失败是独立测试代码问题，不能据此说三组失败全由用户名造成；但这不影响本条关于错误信息缺口的主张。

- 职责与公开约定：E14 明确用户名只允许 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，并要求后续账号变更走 user administration；E73 把该服务的公开错误限定为冲突/密码等六类并建议映射稳定 HTTP 响应，E78 与 E80 也显示 create/update 签名和 400 文档没有用户名格式错误。故字符集/长度是公开契约，缺少对应的可定位错误是改进空间，而不是把愿望写成违约。；证据：E14（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:143–143）、E73（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/references/user-lifecycle-and-deployment.md:24–35）、E78（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.d.ts:43–76）、E80（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/routes/index.js:161–166）
- 实际行为证据：E15/E81 显示 create 仅 trim+lowercase，随后直接 internalAdapter.createUser，catch 只处理唯一约束，其他错误原样抛出；E74 显示认证层使用 Better Auth 默认 username 插件。E16 的实际样例数据运行只输出 `Username is invalid`/`APIError: Username is invalid`；E76 显示旧 fixture 用 it-demo-service 时 Agent 只能推断连字符并改名。应用侧现有路由 schema 也只校验非空（E79），没有前置字符集/长度说明。；证据：E15（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:343–346）、E81（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:96–110）、E74（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:29–31）、E16（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-4.jsonl/part-0025.txt:1150–1170）、E76（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-4.jsonl/part-0025.txt:2167–2168）、E79（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/routes/schemas.js:18–28）
- 应用接入与业务边界：E5 显示应用样例数据确实调用 userManagement.create 并传入 person.username；E76 记录旧 fixture 使用 it-demo-service 等连字符账号，违反 E14 的语法并触发 E16；E77 显示最终改为 itdemo_service 等下划线账号。应用选错用户名是触发条件，但这正是错误信息应帮助定位的场景，改名绕行只修了应用数据，未补框架错误反馈。；证据：E5（app/server/sample-data/it-tickets.ts:61–86）、E76（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-4.jsonl/part-0025.txt:2167–2168）、E14（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:143–143）、E16（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-4.jsonl/part-0025.txt:1150–1170）、E77（app/server/sample-data/it-tickets.ts:13–35）
- 环境因素：失败路径是本地 @nocobase/app-plugin-authentication 的 Better Auth username 校验（E15/E74），不是外部服务、凭据或安装缺失；E16 是本地测试运行的原始输出。按 review-input 的 basis，当前冻结版本为 app-plugin-authentication 2.0.0-beta.4，错误文本来自该路径，不能用最新上游替代。没有证据显示环境把详细错误吞掉；公开 schema/文档本身就没有该信息（E79/E80）。；证据：E15（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:343–346）、E74（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:29–31）、E16（artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-4.jsonl/part-0025.txt:1150–1170）、E79（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/routes/schemas.js:18–28）、E80（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/routes/index.js:161–166）
- 工厂因素：错误发生在包内创建路径（E15/E81）和默认 Better Auth 插件（E74），不是工厂注入配置、QA 或构建脚本；工厂提供的认证 Skill 还明确写出了用户名语法（E14），因此也不是提示缺失。未纳入仓库外 control 脚本，无法排查其他工厂问题；但本条候选没有工厂归因证据。；证据：E15（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:343–346）、E81（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:96–110）、E74（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/auth.js:29–31）、E14（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:143–143）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：公开的 users 创建入口 CreateUserInput 对 username 只要求非空（E79），OpenAPI 400 只列密码/角色错误（E80），UserAdministrationService 的公开错误码也不含用户名格式（E78），实现仅 trim/lowercase（E15）。因此没有现成公开入口能返回允许字符集/长度；需要新增前置校验或把 Better Auth 错误映射为带这些信息的稳定错误。；证据：E79（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/routes/schemas.js:18–28）、E80（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-users/dist/server/routes/index.js:161–166）、E78（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.d.ts:43–76）、E15（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:343–346）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

认证 Skill 明确用户名只能是 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，但 userAdministration.create 的 optionalUsername 只做 trim 和小写化，随后由 Better Auth 抛出不带约束说明的 APIError: Username is invalid。历史修复轮使用带连字符的 it-demo-service 等账号，样例数据测试因此整组失败，最终只能在应用侧改名绕行。

**影响**：调用方无法从错误判断是长度、字符集还是重名；Agent 需要阅读认证插件内部实现或反复试错，增加一轮修复成本，且同样的错误在其他应用中会重复出现。

**建议改动**：在 user-administration 的创建/更新路径按文档语法做前置校验，返回结构化 400（例如 USERNAME_INVALID，并说明允许的字符与长度）；或在捕获 Better Auth 的 username 校验错误时映射为同样的稳定错误。

**验收标准**：新增用例：创建含连字符或超长用户名时返回 400 且 message/字段错误码明确指出允许的 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点；合法用户名仍创建成功。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:143–143` — Skill 明确用户名只允许 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，并说明初始化账号与后续账号都应走用户管理服务。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authentication/dist/server/user-administration.js:343–346` — optionalUsername 只做 trim 和小写化，没有按文档语法校验长度或字符集，随后直接把 username 交给 createUser。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-repair-4.jsonl/part-0025.txt:1150–1170` — 样例数据测试因 Username is invalid 失败，日志同时显示三组测试全部失败；随后 Agent 推断是连字符导致并计划改名。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 39165536643 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。

重要实现缺陷候选反馈：未复核原评审置信度：confirmed
F2 · 工单截图可被任一登录用户枚举和读取

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-file · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

应用把 itTicketFiles 的 read Policy 配成 read.scope: true，元数据路由只加 authentication；内容路由同样只校验登录，没有按 ticketId 对应的 requesterId/assigneeId/服务台角色做记录级授权。文件 Skill 明确要求内容路由前加应用认证与授权，并检查 operation 与记录/父记录 access。

实际影响
任一已登录用户可以获得全部截图的元数据（含 filename、ticketId、uploadedById）并按 UUID 读取原图，绕过工单 own/assigned 数据范围，造成跨用户隐私泄露。
建议改法
在 /api/it-ticket-files 的 list/read 与 /content/* 前安装同一套工单授权：按 ticketId 解析工单 Policy，只允许报修人、被分配工程师和有 allRecords 权限的服务台读取；列表查询也要用 withPolicy/过滤条件收窄，不能只认证。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
任一已登录用户调用工单截图的元数据列表或按 UUID 请求 /api/it-ticket-files/content/<id>，无需是该工单的报修人或被分配人。
应有能力 / 行为
按文件 Skill 的要求，内容与元数据路由都应执行应用授权，并按 operation 与父记录（工单）access 判断；非相关用户应得到 403/404。
实际观察
it-ticket-files 路由只调用 authentication，read Policy 为 read.scope: true；文件插件内容路由本身是公开的且 Policy 不覆盖，因此未见任何工单级限制。
应用绕行与代价
无；应用未提供其他可见性校验，服务台/工程师页面只是客户端过滤，不能阻止直接请求。
改进后的验收标准
用户 B 请求用户 A 工单的截图元数据与内容应返回 403/404；该工单报修人、被分配工程师和服务台可正常读取；列表只返回其可见工单的附件。

实现者过程与运行背景 · 16 个过程问题 / 17 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

Repair 3 fixed the remaining service-desk verification failures: sampleData=yes now creates the demo people and tickets (including the 26-hour-old overdue ticket) because sample user creation no longer mistakes authorization permission sets for application roles; the landing route now shows the waiting/in-progress/overdue/workload overview instead of the template builder introduction; screenshot uploads are checked in the browser and again against stored bytes on the server so renamed text or truncated images cannot be attached; the assignment roster now contains engineers, service-desk members, and root rather than every active employee; the assignment select shows the engineer name instead of a raw UUID while labels load; and the native file input no longer leaves “No file chosen” beside the upload action. Added focused integration and component tests for sample loading, overdue data, screenshot rejection/acceptance, engineer filtering, and the new Home overview. Repair 4 added the missing service-desk operations surface: a Team & settings page where the service desk can list people, create engineer accounts, and replace app permission-set assignments, plus an idempotent demo-data loader that creates independent demo engineers and a 26-hour-old overdue ticket and runs the overdue reminder immediately. It also gives the service desk the user-resource grants its account-management API enforces, and documents one demo engineering account in the page so the browser check can sign in as an engineer and verify assignment isolation and overdue alerts. Focused integration and component tests cover the demo reload, engineer denial, account creation and role replacement, and the add-member dialog.

问题 01 · verification

Fresh employee accounts were denied access to /it-tickets

本任务已处理

A newly registered employee opening /main/it-tickets received 'Access denied: You do not have permission to access it-tickets.'

原因（实现者自述）
The IT permission sets existed, but nothing assigned it-employee to ordinary authenticated users. Only sample users and explicitly assigned accounts had the set, and sample data is not loaded on an already-installed database.
处理方式
Added database/main/seeds/202610070003_it_default_access.ts to assign it-employee to the built-in authenticated:* subject idempotently, matching the built-in member assignment pattern. Added an integration test that creates a normal user and verifies they can list and create tickets and see only their own records.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · verification

Assignment dialog contained no engineers

本任务已处理

The service desk opened the assign dialog and saw 'Only engineers with the IT engineer permission appear here', with an empty list, so the ticket could not be assigned.

原因（实现者自述）
The engineer list was derived only from existing it-engineer permission-set assignments. A fresh installation has no engineer assignments, and sample-data engineers are not loaded unless sample data is installed.
处理方式
The service now lists active people as assignable and accepts any active person. Assignment grants it-engineer to the assignee in the same database transaction as the ticket update, then notifies authorization clients to refresh the assignee's snapshot. Added the root service-desk assignment and root union in service-desk notification recipients.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 03 · verification

Workload section disappeared when no engineer had tickets

本任务已处理

The dashboard had no per-engineer workload, so pending-assignment and overdue counts could not be read alongside who was carrying work.

原因（实现者自述）
The workload card was only rendered when workload entries already existed, which is false immediately after the first assignment grant and on a fresh installation.
处理方式
The dashboard now renders the workload card whenever the caller can assign, with a translated empty state until an engineer has open tickets. The assignment transaction makes the assignee appear in workload immediately.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 04 · verification

Header had no inbox or notification entry

本任务已处理

The authenticated header showed only Appearance and the account menu, with no way to open notifications or follow assignment/resolution links.

原因（实现者自述）
HeaderActions rendered theme settings and the user menu only; the inbox route existed but had no header entry.
处理方式
Installed the official @nocobase/inbox-button composition and added an InboxAction to HeaderActions with unread-count badge, realtime refresh, and document-title badge. It renders only when the API and realtime services are present, preserving minimal hosts and tests. Updated the shell test to assert the inbox link while keeping /inbox out of sidebar navigation.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 05 · verification

Overdue reminders only ran on the next scheduled interval after startup

本任务已处理

Tickets that became older than 24 hours while the application was down waited until the next 15-minute schedule before the service desk was notified.

原因（实现者自述）
The provider registered the recurring job but did not run a reminder pass while starting.
处理方式
The provider now invokes remindOverdue once after schedule setup, logging a warning instead of failing startup if notification delivery is unavailable. The reminder remains deduplicated by overdueNotifiedAt and restores the marker when delivery fails. An integration test moves a ticket older than 24 hours, verifies one notification and no duplicate on the second pass.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 06 · verification

Shell translation test crashed in the header inbox entry

本任务已处理

tests/components/layout-i18n.test.tsx failed with "TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'has')" at client/layouts/components/header-actions.tsx:38.

原因（实现者自述）
HeaderActions assumed useClientApplication() always exposes a service container. The template shell test renders the layout with a minimal app object containing only config and runtime, so app.services was undefined.
处理方式
HeaderActions now treats a missing service container as "inbox unavailable" (optional access) and still checks for the API and realtime tokens when services exist, so minimal hosts and the existing shell test render the rest of the header.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 07 · verification

sampleData=yes produced no demo tickets or overdue example

本任务已处理

The application started with sample data enabled, but the service desk had zero tickets. The required older-than-24-hours ticket therefore could not be demonstrated or used to trigger an overdue reminder.

原因（实现者自述）
server/sample-data/it-tickets.ts created demo users with roleScopes: { app: [permissionSet] }. The users service interprets roleScopes as application roles, not authorization permission sets, so each user creation failed and the sample-data build aborted before creating tickets.
处理方式
Removed roleScopes from sample user creation and relied on the authorization permission-set assignment calls that already follow creation. Added an integration test that starts a fresh app with sampleData: true and asserts all four demo tickets load, exactly one open ticket is older than 24 hours, and the demo engineers receive it-engineer assignments.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 08 · verification

The default Home route was the generic template builder landing page

本任务已处理

Signed-in users landed on a page explaining how to ask a coding agent for features. Pending-assignment, overdue, and engineer workload information was only available after navigating to the IT service desk list.

原因（实现者自述）
client/pages/home/index.tsx still contained the template's capability cards and prompt dialog, and the / route pointed at it unchanged.
处理方式
Replaced the Home component with the service desk overview. It loads the shared dashboard query and renders waiting, in-progress, awaiting-confirmation, overdue, and permission-gated engineer workload sections, with a link to the full ticket queue. Rewrote the Home component test to cover the operational overview and removed assertions for the template introduction from the rendered route.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 09 · verification

Unsupported and corrupt files were accepted as screenshots

本任务已处理

A text file renamed corrupt.png and a file such as unsupported.exe could be selected and uploaded. The UI only opened the picker with accept=image/* and never inspected the selected bytes.

原因（实现者自述）
The create form validated only count and size, the accept attribute is only a picker hint, and the file upload route plus ticket creation validated only MIME prefix, ownership, size, and unclaimed status. No layer checked image signatures or completeness.
处理方式
Added screenshot-validation.ts in the create page to allow PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP, verify the extension and declared MIME type against the file bytes, walk PNG chunks and check JPEG/GIF/WebP end markers, and use createImageBitmap when available. Invalid choices are rejected before upload with a translated field error. Added server/services/it-ticket-image.ts and wired ItTicketService.create through driveManagerToken to re-read stored bytes and reject mismatched or incomplete images before binding files to a ticket. Component tests cover corrupt.png and unsupported.exe; the integration test uploads a corrupt file and receives ATTACHMENT_INVALID with no ticket binding, then accepts a complete PNG.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 10 · verification

Ordinary employees appeared in the Engineer dropdown

本任务已处理

The assignment dialog listed every active signed-in person, including requesters who were not engineers, alongside the actual engineering team.

原因（实现者自述）
ItTicketService.engineers() returned activePeople(), which listed the entire enabled person population rather than the people carrying engineering or service-desk responsibility.
处理方式
The roster now unions it-engineer assignments with it-service-desk assignments and root administrators, then resolves only those enabled person records. Ordinary employees are excluded; assignment still grants it-engineer so a newly chosen engineer becomes part of the roster. The integration test creates a normal employee and asserts they are absent from /tickets/engineers while root remains present for bootstrap assignment.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 11 · verification

The assignment select briefly displayed a raw user UUID

本任务已处理

When reassigning a ticket, the engineer select could show the stored identifier instead of the person's name until the item labels were available.

原因（实现者自述）
The SelectValue had no explicit child, so the Base UI primitive fell back to rendering the selected value string while engineer item labels were still loading or not registered.
处理方式
The assignment select now renders an explicit label from the loaded engineer list, falling back to the ticket's assigneeName (never the UUID) while loading. A component test opens reassignment and asserts the trigger contains Erin Engineer and not engineer-1.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 12 · verification

The upload control showed “No file chosen” after a selection

本任务已处理

The native file input text remained “No file chosen”, implying the selected screenshot had not been attached even though a chip was shown.

原因（实现者自述）
changeFiles() reset event.target.value to an empty string so the same file could be chosen again, which also reset the native control's displayed filename. The form used the browser's default file input appearance.
处理方式
The native input is now visually hidden and the form presents a translated Choose screenshots / Add more screenshots button that opens it. Selected files remain visible as removable chips, and resetting the hidden input no longer affects the visible control. The upload component test asserts the button changes to Add more screenshots after a valid selection.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 13 · verification

No independent engineer accounts or account-management surface

本任务已处理

The assignment dropdown contained only 'Super Admin · admin@nocobase.com'. The sidebar had only Home and IT service desk, so a verifier could not create another person or grant the IT engineer role, and engineer-only visibility could not be exercised.

原因（实现者自述）
The install starts with only the root administrator. Ordinary sign-ups receive the it-employee permission set, the assignment roster is intentionally restricted to it-engineer, it-service-desk, and root, and there was no page or API surface in the application for creating accounts or assigning permission sets. Assignment granted it-engineer only after a person was already selected, so there was no way to bootstrap an engineer through the product.
处理方式
Added the permission-gated Team & settings page (/it-team) with the current roster, role replacement through PUT /api/users/:userId/roleScopes/app, an add-member dialog that creates an account with an explicit permission-set role, and a merge-only seed granting the service desk the it.team page plus user read/create/assign-role actions. The assignment roster keeps its engineer/service-desk/root restriction, and the demo loader also creates two independent engineers.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 14 · verification

No ticket older than 24 hours, so the overdue reminder could not be exercised

本任务已处理

The dashboard always showed Overdue 0, no ticket was older than 24 hours, and the create form cannot change createdAt, so the verifier could not observe or test the reminder path.

原因（实现者自述）
The browser installation is created without APP_SAMPLE_DATA. The demo seed is declared with sample: true, which is recorded as skipped in that mode, and sample mode is first-start-only. The application therefore had no aged fixture even though the demo seed contains one.
处理方式
Added a manage-gated, idempotent POST /api/tickets/sample-data endpoint and a Team page action that loads the same demo people and tickets on demand. The loader creates a 26-hour-old in-progress ticket assigned to a demo engineer, runs remindOverdue immediately so the service-desk notification exists without waiting for the schedule, and does not duplicate records on repeat calls. Integration tests assert the aged ticket, the reload idempotency, and the reminder count.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 15 · verification

Service desk account management was denied by the user resource

本任务已处理

The new Team page's GET /api/users call returned 403 AuthorizationDeniedError: No grant allows user:*.read for the demo service-desk user, so the roster and add-member flow could not work.

原因（实现者自述）
The it-service-desk permission set originally held only ticket grants. The Users API enforces its own user-record actions (read, create, assign-role) independently of the Team page grant, and the application had not granted those actions to the service desk.
处理方式
Added idempotent seed 202610070004_it_team_access.ts, which merges the it.team page grant, user read/create/assign-role actions, and the composite it.tickets manage action into the existing service-desk set without replacing administrator changes. Integration tests now verify that the service desk can list and create users and replace a role scope, while an engineer receives 403.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 16 · verification

Demo usernames containing hyphens were rejected at creation

本任务已处理

Loading demo data failed with APIError: Username is invalid when the seed used identifiers such as it-demo-service.

原因（实现者自述）
Better Auth's username validation accepts letters, digits, underscores, and dots and rejects hyphens, but the demo fixtures had been written with readable hyphenated usernames.
处理方式
Changed the demo usernames to itdemo_service, itdemo_engineer1, itdemo_engineer2, and itdemo_employee, kept the matching email addresses usable for sign-in, and displayed one engineer and one employee account on the Team page so the verifier knows which independent accounts to use.
影响 / 代价：未提供，不估算来源：Agent · retro.json

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · 验收机制

Exercise a fresh non-sample account in end-to-end checks

可自动化

The verification failure came from relying on sample users and pre-existing assignments. The repaired test suite now creates a normal user, verifies default page access and own-record scope, assigns that user, and walks progress, resolve, return, close, notification links, and overdue deduplication.

建议改动

Keep at least one integration scenario that starts without sample data and creates its users through the public Users API.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · authorization

Pair baseline permission seeds with a fresh-account test

可自动化

Permission sets alone do not make product pages reachable; an authenticated baseline assignment is what gives every signed-in person access. This is easy to miss when sample data supplies the assignments during development.

建议改动

When adding a page permission set for all employees, add or verify the authenticated:* assignment and assert the page and action behavior with a newly created user.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 03 · assignment

Do not require a pre-seeded role before the first assignment

需人工判断

A service desk cannot bootstrap a team if the assignee list only contains people who already hold the engineer role. Granting the role as part of assignment keeps the first assignment possible and the notification target usable.

建议改动

Model first assignment as an atomic ticket update plus permission grant, and list active people rather than role holders.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 04 · ui

Render empty operational panels instead of hiding them

可自动化

A dashboard section that disappears when its data is empty looks like a missing feature during verification. An explicit empty state preserves the information architecture and explains what will appear.

建议改动

For permission-gated operational panels, render the panel with a translated empty state rather than conditionally hiding it on zero rows.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 05 · ui

Do not assume optional client services exist in shell components

可自动化

The inbox entry was gated on services registered by plugins, but the gate itself dereferenced the container unconditionally and broke a shell test that renders the layout without one.

建议改动

When a shell component depends on plugin-registered services, read the container optionally and degrade to the base header instead of requiring the full runtime.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 06 · 验收机制

Exercise sample data on a fresh database, not only service paths

可自动化

Sample-data failures are logged and the application still starts, so a missing demo set can look like an empty application instead of a broken seed. The invalid roleScopes call was only visible by starting with sampleData: true and querying the resulting tickets.

建议改动

Keep a fresh-app integration test that enables sample data and asserts the demo records, permission-set assignments, and the overdue example exist.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 07 · database

Do not pass authorization permission-set keys as application role scopes

可自动化

The users service and the authorization plugin model different concepts. roleScopes binds application roles; permission-set assignments are made through the authorization API. Mixing them makes user creation fail before downstream seed work runs.

建议改动

When seeding users, create the account first and assign permission sets through authz.permissionSets.assign, rather than putting permission-set keys in roleScopes.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 08 · security

Treat a file picker's accept list as a hint and validate bytes on both sides

可自动化

An accept attribute filters the dialog but does not guarantee the chosen file, and a browser-supplied MIME type can be renamed or falsified. Client validation improves feedback; server validation is what keeps invalid data out of the database.

建议改动

For uploads whose type matters, validate extension, declared type, signatures, and completeness in the browser, then re-read the stored bytes in the service before binding them to a business record.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 09 · ui

Do not reveal raw identifiers while selection labels load

可自动化

A generic select that falls back to its value renders a UUID before item registration. Passing an explicit label or the business record's display name keeps the control readable throughout loading.

建议改动

For record-backed selects, render an explicit label from loaded data with a business-name fallback and assert in a component test that the raw id never appears.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 10 · ui

Hide native file inputs when their default copy conflicts with the action

可自动化

Resetting a native file input to allow repeated selection also resets its filename text, producing a misleading “No file chosen” state. A custom trigger plus separate file chips separates the action from selection state.

建议改动

Use a visually hidden input, a translated button that opens it, and an explicit selected-file list; reset the hidden input without changing the visible copy.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 11 · product

Make the landing route an operational overview, not template education

需人工判断

A generated application's default page should answer the question its users arrive with. Leaving the builder introduction on / hides the product's actual status information behind another navigation step.

建议改动

When adapting a template, replace the landing page with the product's primary overview and keep the template copy out of the reachable route.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 12 · product

Give an installed product an operations surface, not only seeded records

需人工判断

A fresh install could show the queue but not create the people who run it or demonstrate aged work, because the only bootstrap path was sample data the deployment did not enable. The feature had assumed a pre-seeded team. The Team & settings page and explicit demo-data action now provide a normal in-product path.

建议改动

For each role that a workflow depends on, provide an in-product path to create or assign that role and to load an idempotent demonstration fixture; do not make first-run operability depend on APP_SAMPLE_DATA.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 13 · authorization

Pair a role-filtered roster with a way to grant the role

可自动化

Restricting assignees to it-engineer made the first assignment impossible when root was the only user, and the new Team page still needed separate user-resource grants. The roster filter stays, but the Team page can grant it-engineer and the seed adds the user actions the Users API checks.

建议改动

When a list is filtered by a role, ship the role-assignment surface and its record-level grants in the same change, and test both the page and its API as the target role.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 14 · 验收机制

Test first-run behavior with sample mode disabled

可自动化

Sample-data tests passed while the browser install had no demo users or aged ticket, because the tests enabled sample mode and the browser harness did not. The demo loader and role boundaries are now tested through HTTP without relying on startup sample mode.

建议改动

Keep at least one integration flow that starts from a non-sample install, then creates its people and fixture through public product endpoints.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 15 · authorization

Test the role's real API grants, not only its page grant

可自动化

The Team page page grant did not make GET /api/users pass; the Users API checks user:*.read separately. Page access, composite actions, and plugin record actions are independent checks, so the seed now grants each user action and integration tests cover allowed and denied responses.

建议改动

For a new page, enumerate its API calls, grant each checked resource/action, and assert allowed and denied responses for the intended role and an unrelated role.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 16 · database

Make operational bootstrap seeds merge-only and idempotent

可自动化

Permission sets are stored as a whole JSON value and demo fixtures create records, so extending grants or rerunning the loader could overwrite administrator changes or duplicate data. Seed 004 merges actions by resource key, and the loader finds people by email and tickets by title before creating anything.

建议改动

For operational seeds that extend existing configuration, merge by resource/action key and test a second application; for demo records, assert the second load leaves counts unchanged.

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 17 · 验收机制

Use authentication-compatible fixture identifiers

可自动化

Demo data failed until hyphenated usernames were replaced because Better Auth rejects hyphens. Fixture identifiers must satisfy the authentication provider's username grammar; the demo accounts now use letters, digits, and underscores, and the Team page displays them.

建议改动

Validate fixture usernames against the authentication plugin's accepted format in an integration test that signs in with each demo account.

来源：Agent · retro.json

本次 IT 工单场景中，NocoBase3 的迁移/种子、授权、通知、定时任务和文件能力均有公开入口并被实际接入，最终 verify-5 的 C01 通过；仍有两项可行动问题：应用把截图内容路由只加了登录校验，任一登录用户可枚举并读取全部工单附件（应用归因、major），以及账号创建对非法用户名只返回泛化的 Username is invalid，而创建服务未按文档语法前置校验（插件易用性、minor）。框架侧的直接能力总体可用，主要成本集中在授权与文件路由需要应用完整装配，未发现需要自建基础能力的缺口。

用可复现的迁移创建工单、活动和截图元数据表及索引直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供迁移定义、集合/字段/索引构建 API 与发现机制；表结构和业务字段由应用声明。
推荐使用方式
按 Skill 在 database/main/migrations 中 defineMigration，并在 up 中用 builder.createCollection 声明字段和索引，down 中按逆序删除。
Agent 实际使用
应用迁移逐项使用 createCollection 声明 3 个集合，包含 File 契约列和业务外键列，并建立 status/assigneeId 等索引；写法与 Skill 示例一致。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

框架提供事务化 seed 上下文和 query；权限集内容与幂等键由应用负责。
推荐使用方式
用 defineSeed({ transaction: true }) 并在 run 中先查后插，以稳定 key 作为幂等键。
Agent 实际使用
应用种子使用 transaction: true，按 permissionSet.key 查询后插入，重复运行会跳过已存在记录。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

按报修人/被分配人限制工单可见范围直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 recordAccess、复合资源和 Policy 绑定；哪些角色看哪些范围由应用声明。
推荐使用方式

E9 E11 E12
每个服务动作先授权，拒绝时不得读写记录直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 context.authorize 与 Policy 条件；业务动作与状态机由应用负责。
推荐使用方式
在 authz middleware 后调用一次 authorize，检查 deny 与 database policy，再用 withPolicy 绑定所有读写。
Agent 实际使用
ItTicketService.policy 调用 context.authorize({ resource: composite it.tickets, action })，deny 或缺 policy 时抛 403，随后用 withPolicy 查询。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E10 E11 E12

推荐使用方式

在应用配置中把 channels.inbox 映射到 in-app provider，解析 notificationServiceToken 后按频道构造消息。
Agent 实际使用
应用配置 channels.inbox = { provider: in-app }，service provider 解析 notificationServiceToken 并注入 notifier。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E21 E25 E20
同一业务事件重复发送不得产生重复通知直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架校验 idempotencyKey 并持久化投递；键的业务唯一性由应用定义。
推荐使用方式
每条逻辑通知用稳定 idempotencyKey，并在 source.referenceId 保留来源引用。
Agent 实际使用
分配/创建/关闭/超时用稳定键，进展/解决/退回为允许重复的业务事件使用时间戳键；send 同时传 source.type 和 referenceId。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E20 E23 E25
点击站内信能回到对应工单详情直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架定义并校验 route/url target，in-app 频道渲染站内跳转。
推荐使用方式
使用 { type: route, path: /it-tickets/<id> }，路径必须以单斜杠开头且不含空白。
Agent 实际使用
所有工单通知都使用 /it-tickets/<id> 的 route target；in-app 组件对 route target 渲染 NavLink。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E20 E22 E24
通知失败不能回滚已经提交的工单变更直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架通知服务独立于业务事务；应用需隔离失败。
推荐使用方式
在业务事务提交后发送，或捕获通知异常并记录，不让投递失败传播为业务失败。
Agent 实际使用
notifier.send 捕获异常、logger.warn 并返回 false；工单服务在事务提交后调用通知。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E20 E25
未读数量和浏览器标题反映待办直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供 inbox 数据与 Client 组件；徽标优先级属于应用 UI 规则。
推荐使用方式
使用 in-app 组件的未读查询与 mutation，应用可组合 badge/title。
Agent 实际使用
应用实现 decisions 优先于 unread 的徽标和标题前缀，in-app 组件提供未读/已读 mutation。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

E30

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

每 15 分钟自动扫描超时工单直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供进程内 ScheduleExecutor 与 every/cron 调度；扫描条件由应用定义。
推荐使用方式
从 jobExecutorServiceToken 取本应用 scope 的 executor，addJob 后 setup，execute 只调用一个服务方法。
Agent 实际使用
provider.start 取 it-service-desk executor，addJob({ every: 15*60*1000 })，execute 调用 service.remindOverdue，随后 setup。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E27 E29 E31
同一工单超时只提醒一次，重启后不重复直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架保证任务规则持久化和单实例消费；提醒幂等由应用字段与条件保证。
推荐使用方式
在业务表记录 notifiedAt，查询时要求为空，发送成功后再写入；失败则回退标记。
Agent 实际使用
remindOverdue 过滤 overdueNotifiedAt 为空，更新后再发送，发送失败把标记清回 null；overdue 通知使用稳定 idempotencyKey。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E32 E27
启动、重启和关闭时任务生命周期正确直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架在 setup 时写规则并启动消费，shutdown 停止本实例而不删除规则。
推荐使用方式
在 start 中 addJob 后 setup，在 shutdown 中 await executor.shutdown()。
Agent 实际使用
provider.start 在 addJob 后 await setup，shutdown 调用 executor.shutdown；应用还在 start 末尾立即补扫一次。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E28 E29 E31
不需要管理员在 UI 跟踪的任务不应占用 Scheduler 插件直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架用 Scheduler 服务可见任务、用私有 ScheduleExecutor 服务后台任务；选择边界由指引说明。
推荐使用方式
无人查看的后台扫描使用 jobExecutorServiceToken 的私有 executor，只有需要 UI 的任务才注册 Scheduler。
Agent 实际使用
应用没有注册 Scheduler 计划，而是使用私有 it-service-desk executor，符合指引。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

E36 E37

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

建立符合 File 契约的附件集合直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架规定固定 File 列与上传 Policy 派生规则；集合名和业务关联列由应用声明。
推荐使用方式
按 Skill 在迁移中逐列声明 id/disk/key/filename/ext/mimeType/size/createdAt/updatedAt，再追加业务关联列。
Agent 实际使用

缺口归因

E35 E36
只接受完整可解码的 PNG/JPEG/GIF/WebP，且不超过 5MB直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
框架提供上传大小/类型限制和 Policy；业务级字节完整性校验可由应用补充。
推荐使用方式
使用 exposure 的 maxSize/mime 限制并在提交时校验文件归属、大小和类型；需要更强校验时在应用侧读字节。
Agent 实际使用
应用在浏览器按 magic bytes、IEND/EOI 等结构校验并尝试解码，服务端再次检查归属、大小、image/* 和文件字节。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E38 E39 E40
附件只能被工单报修人、被分配工程师和服务台查看需要绕行
框架职责与业务边界
文件插件的 API/内容路由默认公开，框架要求应用在自有路径上注册认证与授权；工单可见性规则属于应用。
推荐使用方式
在 /<name>/<action> 与 <accessPath>/* 前加应用认证/授权，并按 operation 与记录/父记录 access 检查；不能只靠登录或 Policy 字段白名单。
Agent 实际使用
应用给元数据路由加了 authentication，但 read Policy 使用 read.scope: true；内容路由只有 authentication，没有按 ticketId/requesterId/assigneeId 的记录授权。
缺口归因
Agent / 业务实现
E33 E34 E36 E37
上传后可预览和下载原图直接支持
框架职责与业务边界

推荐使用方式
通过 accessPath/<uuid>.<ext> 读取内容，按磁盘能力选择 stream 或 redirect。
Agent 实际使用
应用生成 /api/it-ticket-files/content/<id> 路由并读取 drive 字节，客户端用该地址显示图片。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202610070001_create_it_tickets',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('itTickets', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('title', { length: 120 }).notNull();
      collection.text('description').notNull();
      collection
        .string('urgency', { length: 16 })
        .notNull()
        .defaultTo('medium');
      collection
        .string('status', { length: 24 })
        .notNull()
        .defaultTo('pending');
      collection.string('requesterId', { length: 64 }).notNull();
      collection.string('assigneeId', { length: 64 }).nullable();
      collection.text('resolution').nullable();
      collection.datetime('statusChangedAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('assignedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('resolvedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('closedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('returnedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('overdueNotifiedAt').nullable();
      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();

      collection.index('status');
      collection.index('requesterId');
      collection.index('assigneeId');
      collection.index('createdAt');
      collection.index(['status', 'createdAt']);

/** Marks and returns a migration definition for discovery by the migration loader. */

E4 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/collection/builder/builder.js:85–88

builder.createCollection 归一化输入后通过 apply 执行 createCollection 操作，是迁移建表的实际入口。

    async createCollection(name, input, options = {}) {
        const definition = normalizeCollectionInput(input);
        return this.apply([{ type: 'createCollection', name, definition }], options);
    }

        name: person.name,

        password=[REDACTED],
      });

      person.permissionSet,
    );

      !assignments.some(
        (assignment) =>
          assignment.subject.type === 'user' &&
          assignment.subject.id === userId,
      )
    ) {
      await context.authz.permissionSets.assign({
        subject: { type: 'user', id: userId },
        permissionSet: person.permissionSet,
      });
    }

E6 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:147–147

Skill 说明跨插件服务的样例数据应在 ServiceProvider.boot() 注册到 sampleDataToken，并给出 register({ name, packageName, run }) 形式。

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.

E8 · app/database/main/seeds/202610070002_it_permission_sets.ts:78–99

种子在 transaction: true 下按 permissionSet.key 查询已存在记录，再插入授权集，形成稳定幂等键。

  async run({ query }) {
    for (const permissionSet of permissionSets) {
      const existing = await query
        .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSets')
        .select('id')
        .where('key', '=', permissionSet.key)
        .executeTakeFirst();
      if (existing) continue;

      const now = new Date();
      await query
        .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSets')
        .values({
          id: permissionSet.key,
          key: permissionSet.key,
          title: encodeAuthorizationTitle(permissionSet.title),
          grants: JSON.stringify(permissionSet.grants),
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        })
        .execute();
    }

E9 · app/server/it-ticket-resources.ts:71–108

应用定义 it.tickets.own 与 it.tickets.assigned 两个 recordAccess，并让 ticketRead 默认使用 own 范围。

/** A ticket requested by the signed-in user. */
export const itTicketsOwn = defineRecordAccess('it.tickets.own', (access) =>
  access
    .title('Requested by me')
    .collections('itTickets')
    .resolver(({ principal }) =>
      principal.type === 'user'
        ? buildFilter((f) => f.string('requesterId').eq(principal.id))
        : false,
    ),
);

/** A ticket assigned to the signed-in engineer. */
export const itTicketsAssigned = defineRecordAccess(
  'it.tickets.assigned',
  (access) =>
    access
      .title('Assigned to me')
      .collections('itTickets')
      .resolver(({ principal }) =>
        principal.type === 'user'
          ? buildFilter((f) => f.string('assigneeId').eq(principal.id))
          : false,
      ),
);

const ticketRead = defineDatabasePermission((p) =>
  p
    .collection('itTickets')
    .title('IT tickets')
    .read(IT_TICKET_READ_FIELDS)
    .options(
      itTicketsOwn.reference(),
      itTicketsAssigned.reference(),
      recordAccess.allRecords,
    )
    .default(itTicketsOwn.reference()),
);

E10 · app/server/services/it-ticket-service.ts:141–168

服务先 context.authorize 复合资源动作，deny 或缺 database.itTickets policy 时抛 403，否则返回 Policy 供后续 withPolicy 使用。

  private async policy(
    context: AuthorizationContext,
    action: TicketAction,
  ): Promise<TicketPolicy> {
    const decision = await context.authorize({
      resource: { type: 'composite', id: 'it.tickets' },
      action,
    });
    if (decision.effect === 'deny') {
      throw new ItTicketError(
        'FORBIDDEN',
        403,
        'You may not perform this action',
      );
    }
    const database = (
      decision.conditions as { database?: Record<string, TicketPolicy> }
    ).database;
    const policy = database?.itTickets;
    if (!policy) {
      throw new ItTicketError(
        'FORBIDDEN',
        403,
        'You may not perform this action',
      );
    }
    return policy;
  }

E11 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authorization/SKILL.md:57–62

Skill 要求用 defineDatabasePermission/defineCompositeResource 组合权限，并在多范围或持久状态场景安装 authz.middleware、调用 authorize、用 withPolicy 绑定查询。

1. Declare schema and relationships through self-contained migrations. Choose stable composite, page and data scope names. Keep authorization decisions out of migration code.
2. Declare reusable collection permissions with `defineDatabasePermission`; compose user-facing business operations as composite resources with `defineCompositeResource` from `@nocobase/authorization/core`. Explicitly name fields, relation capabilities and every collection a workflow touches.
3. In the owning provider's `boot`, opt collections in with `authz.database.collections.add`, register composites with `authz.compositeResources.define`, record access with `authz.recordAccess.define` and settings items with `authz.settings.add`. Then add a workspace subsection with `authz.ui.sections.add({ name, title, parent })` and list each composite or settings item in it with `authz.ui.place(reference, { section, group? })` (the permission workspace lists sections and subsections on the left and the selected subsection's resources, under optional `authz.ui.groups`, on the right; an unplaced composite or settings item goes under its default section's Other with a startup warning, and a misplaced one fails a development start). Pages are not registered on the server: the client route tree lists them. Release subject registrations at shutdown.
4. Protect every server route with authentication. For a simple single-scope Repository operation, keep `defineRepositoryApiRoutes` and add `authz.database.authorizeRepository` to map methods to composite actions; it reuses or initializes the request's authorization context. For workflows, persisted-state checks, multiple data scopes or custom responses, install `authz.middleware()`, call `c.get('authz').authorize()` once and bind every returned collection policy with `repository.withPolicy()`. Follow [Repository route integration](references/repository-routes.md).
5. Declare `authz` on every entry client route (nested pages inherit it; an omitted one defaults to root-only or `'skip'` with a development warning), add `useCan` for action visibility and obtain per-record eligibility from the server when needed. Handle pending and error states without displaying stale access.
6. Complete the accompanying permission configuration from the responsibility matrix: sets, page and action grants, selected data scopes, supported rules and intended assignments. Use seeds for fresh installations and authorized provisioning for existing installations; editing a seed does not update a running App. Resolve optional capabilities through their installed Skills and preserve unrelated administrator edits.

原文件 SHA-256：2926bec370b7b9fe18232839cd91455a8af5f99381e551bd46323fa21202b966

E12 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-authorization/dist/server/authorize-repository.js:14–36

createCompositeRepositoryAuthorization 校验 Repository 的每个方法都绑定单集合、单范围的复合动作，并检查对应 grant 的数据库操作。

export function createCompositeRepositoryAuthorization(authz, options) {
    if (!options.repository || options.repository.includes('*'))
        throw new TypeError('Repository name must be non-empty and contain no wildcard');
    const repository = options.repository;
    const resource = options.resource.name;
    const bindings = new Map();
    for (const [method, action] of Object.entries(options.actions)) {
        if (!Object.hasOwn(operations, method) || typeof action !== 'string')
            throw new TypeError('Invalid Repository action binding');
        const definition = authz.compositeResources.getAction(resource, action);
        const scopes = definition?.dataScopes ?? [];
        const target = definition && scopes.length === 1
            ? dataScopeTarget(definition, scopes[0].key)
            : undefined;
        const collection = target?.type === 'database.collection' ? target.id : undefined;
        if (!definition ||
            !collection ||
            definition.grants.some((grant) => grant.resource.type !== 'database.collection' ||
                grant.resource.id !== collection) ||
            !definition.grants.some((grant) => grant.actions.some((entry) => entry.action === operations[method]))) {
            throw new TypeError(`Repository ${repository}:${method} requires a single-collection, single-scope composite action with the matching database operation`);
        }
        bindings.set(method, { action, collection });

E13 · app/database/main/seeds/202610070003_it_default_access.ts:14–37

默认访问种子以 authenticated:* 为 subject 分配 it-employee，并保持幂等插入。

export default defineSeed({
  name: '202610070003_it_default_access',
  transaction: true,
  async run({ query }) {
    const now = new Date();
    const assignmentId = 'authenticated:*:it-employee';
    const existingEmployee = await query
      .selectFrom('authorizationPermissionSetAssignments')
      .select('id')
      .where('id', '=', assignmentId)
      .executeTakeFirst();
    if (!existingEmployee) {
      await query
        .insertInto('authorizationPermissionSetAssignments')
        .values({
          id: assignmentId,
          subjectType: 'authenticated',
          subjectId: '*',
          permissionSetKey: 'it-employee',
          createdAt: now,
          updatedAt: now,
        })
        .execute();
    }

E14 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-authentication/SKILL.md:143–143

Skill 明确用户名只允许 3–30 位字母、数字、下划线或点，并说明初始化账号与后续账号都应走用户管理服务。

Before the first seed run, set `users.initialAdmin.username`, `users.initialAdmin.email` and `users.initialAdmin.password` in application configuration to customize the bootstrap account. Omitting the entire node preserves `nocobase` / `admin@nocobase.com` / `admin123`; an explicit node requires a nonempty password and defaults the username to `nocobase` and the email to `admin@nocobase.com`. Usernames contain 3–30 letters, digits, underscores or dots, emails must be valid addresses, and both are stored lowercase. Initialization only runs when the user table is empty, hashes the password and grants the newly created account root permission through the authorization plugin. After initialization, configuration changes do not reset accounts or rerun the recorded seeds. The root permission seed selects the administrator by the same configured username. Use the user administration service for subsequent account changes. Do not edit historical seeds or add a competing administrator seed.

E19 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/SKILL.md:22–34

Skill 列出 userManagementServiceToken 等服务器契约，并要求按 user 记录类型授予所需 action，插件本身不创建角色。

- Server contracts: `userManagementServiceToken`,
  `userRoleScopeRegistryToken`, `UserRoleScope`, and related types from
  `@nocobase/app-plugin-users/server/tokens`.
- HTTP API: `GET /api/users` (`page`, `pageSize`, `q`, `status`, `roleScope`, `role`; answers `{ data, meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`), `GET /api/users/options`, `POST /api/users`, `PATCH /api/users/:userId`, `DELETE /api/users/:userId?confirm=true`, `POST /api/users/:userId/disable|enable|resetPassword|revokeSessions`, and `PUT /api/users/:userId/roleScopes/:scope`. The running application documents each one, with request and response schemas, at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`) under the `Users` tag. Failures use the standard error body; branch on `error.reason`, such as `USER_NOT_FOUND`, `SELF_DELETE_NOT_ALLOWED`, `LAST_ASSIGNMENT` or `USER_EMAIL_CONFLICT`.

Every HTTP route requires Authentication and Authorization. Routes check the `user` record type with one of `read`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `assign-role`, `reset-password`, `revoke-sessions` or `delete`. Account creation checks both `create` and `assign-role`. `delete` is permitted only while an application role scope can clean a deleted user up.

## Register the plugin

1. Register Authentication, Authorization, and then Users in the App's Client
   and Server plugin arrays. The Client factory takes no placement options:
   `users()`.
2. Grant only the `user` actions those roles need, on `{ type: 'user', id: '*' }` because `user` is a record type. The plugin creates no roles and grants no access by itself.

E22 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/dist/shared/target.js:1–32

target 校验只接受单斜杠开头且无控制字符/反斜杠的 route，或完整 HTTP(S) 且无凭据的 url，并返回规范化 target。

export function isNotificationTarget(value) {
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'object')
        return false;
    const target = value;
    if (target.type === 'route') {
        return (typeof target.path === 'string' &&
            target.path.startsWith('/') &&
            !target.path.startsWith('//') &&
            !hasUnsafeCharacters(target.path));
    }
    if (target.type !== 'url' ||
        typeof target.url !== 'string' ||
        !/^https?:\/\//i.test(target.url) ||
        hasUnsafeCharacters(target.url))
        return false;
    try {
        const url = new URL(target.url);
        return Boolean(url.hostname) && !url.username && !url.password;
    }
    catch {
        return false;
    }
}
export function validateNotificationTarget(value) {
    if (value === undefined || value === null)
        return undefined;
    if (!isNotificationTarget(value))
        throw new Error('Invalid notification target: use an internal route path or a complete HTTP(S) URL.');
    return value.type === 'route'
        ? { type: 'route', path: value.path }
        : { type: 'url', url: value.url };
}

E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-notification/dist/server/manager.js:211–220

NotificationService.send 先激活服务并校验 idempotencyKey，再校验 source 的 type 和可选 referenceId，非法输入抛错。

    async send(input) {
        await this.activate();
        validateNotificationIdempotencyKey(input.idempotencyKey);
        if (input.source !== undefined &&
            (!input.source ||
                typeof input.source.type !== 'string' ||
                !input.source.type.trim() ||
                (input.source.referenceId !== undefined &&
                    typeof input.source.referenceId !== 'string')))
            throw new Error('Notification source requires a type and an optional string referenceId.');

E25 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-notification/SKILL.md:19–24

Skill 要求解析 notificationServiceToken、按 Channel 构造完整消息，并保留投递/重试历史、区分受理与最终投递。

1. Read [Notification concepts](references/notification-concepts.md) for package ownership and delivery status semantics.
2. For plugin registration, configuration, migrations, or test APIs, read [Integration and configuration](references/integration-and-configuration.md). Configure a Channel name mapped to one flat Provider configuration.
3. For sending or typed consumers, read [Sending notifications](references/sending-notifications.md). Resolve `notificationServiceToken`; construct complete messages keyed by Channel name and use native recipient addresses. Validate the intended external effect before sending.
4. For failures and retries, read [Delivery diagnostics](references/delivery-diagnostics.md). Preserve Notification, Delivery, Attempt, and retry-audit history; distinguish accepted submission from final delivery.
5. For custom transports, read [Channel and Provider extensions](references/channel-and-provider-extensions.md). Register globally unique Provider definitions through `notificationExtensionRegistryToken` and retain the queue/lease/retry lifecycle.
6. Verify changed packages and consumers with lint, typecheck, tests, and build. Cover atomic validation, independent delivery, recipient rules, and retries bound to original identities.

E26 · app/client/components/inbox-badge.ts:11–28

应用定义 decisions 优先于 unread 的徽标规则，并复用框架 inbox 的未读数据。

export type InboxBadge = {
  readonly kind: 'decisions' | 'unread';
  readonly count: number;
  readonly text: string;
} | null;

/** The badge's text for `count`: "99+" above 99 so the badge keeps its size, none at zero. */
export function inboxBadgeText(count: number): string | null {
  if (!Number.isFinite(count) || count <= 0) return null;
  return count > 99 ? '99+' : String(count);
}

export function inboxBadge(decisions: number, unread: number): InboxBadge {
  const pending = inboxBadgeText(decisions);
  if (pending) return { kind: 'decisions', count: decisions, text: pending };
  const text = inboxBadgeText(unread);
  return text ? { kind: 'unread', count: unread, text } : null;
}

原文件 SHA-256：c81d862e2301a8d4097c4cdbf5bf524b8d53c05c5c262ebe2af5a7d2f7234490

E27 · packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/schedule/executor.js:34–52

addJob 归一化任务并保存 handler；created 状态先放入 pending，等 setup 统一 upsert，避免规则先于 handler 生效。

    async addJob(job, registerOnly = false) {
        this.assertOpen();
        const normalized = normalizeScheduleJob(job);
        this.handlers.set(normalized.name, job);
        if (registerOnly) {
            this.pending.delete(normalized.name);
            return {
                jobName: normalized.name,
                code: ScheduleReceiptCode.HandlerRegistered,
                message: 'Handler registered',
            };
        }
        if (this.state === 'created') {
            // Nothing is written before setup(): the rule is written there, before
            // any worker starts, so a due firing never meets a missing handler.
            this.pending.set(normalized.name, {
                job: normalized,
                payload: job.payload,
            });

E28 · packages/@nocobase/jobs/dist/schedule/executor.js:104–126

setup 缓存进行中的 setupPromise，runSetup 打开 backend、写入 pending 规则并开始消费；shutdown 缓存关闭流程。

    setup(options = {}) {
        this.assertOpen();
        this.setupPromise ??= this.runSetup(options.consume ?? true);
        return this.setupPromise;
    }
    shutdown() {
        this.shutdownPromise ??= this.runShutdown();
        return this.shutdownPromise;
    }
    async runSetup(consume) {
        this.state = 'setting-up';
        try {
            await this.backend.open();
            this.opened = true;
            for (const { job, payload } of this.pending.values()) {
                await this.upsert(job, payload);
            }
            this.pending.clear();
            if (consume)
                await this.backend.consume(this.runner);
            // shutdown() may have been called while this ran; it closes what was opened.
            if (this.state === 'setting-up')
                this.state = 'ready';

E29 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/services-and-jobs.md:136–168

指引给出解析 jobExecutorServiceToken、getScheduleExecutor(scope)、addJob、setup 和 shutdown 的完整 provider 示例，并说明规则持久化与 execute 应保持轻薄。

Resolve `jobExecutorServiceToken` and ask it for an executor of your own, with your package name as the scope. Register the jobs, then call `setup()`, both in `start()`, and shut the executor down in `shutdown()`:

```ts
import { jobExecutorServiceToken } from '@nocobase/app-server/jobs';
import type { ScheduleExecutor } from '@nocobase/jobs';

export default class OverdueScanProvider extends ServiceProvider<Application> {
  public readonly name: string = 'app/overdue-scan-provider';

  private executor: ScheduleExecutor | undefined;

  public override async start(): Promise<void> {
    this.executor = this.app.container
      .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
      .getScheduleExecutor('@acme/crm');
    await this.executor.addJob({
      name: 'overdue-scan',
      options: { cron: '0 8 * * *', tz: 'Asia/Shanghai' },
      payload: {},
      execute: async ({ jobId, signal }) => {
        // Keep this thin: resolve the service and call it.
      },
    });
    await this.executor.setup();
  }

  public override async shutdown(): Promise<void> {
    await this.executor?.shutdown();
  }
}
```

The rule is stored, not just held in memory: `addJob` writes it on `setup()` and leaves it alone when nothing changed, and `shutdown()` stops this instance without removing it. `removeJob(name)` deletes a job you no longer define; `getJob` and `listJob` show when each fires next. A job's `name` must be stable and may not contain `:`. `cron` takes five or six fields, `tz` defaults to UTC, and `every`, `limit`, `startDate` and `endDate` are the other options. The types come from `@nocobase/jobs`, which the application already depends on.

  public override async start(): Promise<void> {
    const container = this.app.container;
    if (
      !container.has(itTicketServiceToken) ||
      !container.has(jobExecutorServiceToken)
    ) {
      return;
    }
    const service = container.resolve(itTicketServiceToken);
    this.executor = container
      .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
      .getScheduleExecutor(IT_TICKET_SCHEDULE_SCOPE);
    await this.executor.addJob({
      name: 'overdue-ticket-reminder',
      options: { every: 15 * 60 * 1000 },
      payload: {},
      execute: async () => {
        await service.remindOverdue();
      },
    });
    await this.executor.setup();
    // Remind about tickets that became overdue while the application was down
    // instead of waiting for the first interval to elapse.
    try {
      await service.remindOverdue();
    } catch (error) {
      container
        .resolve(loggingToken)
        .getLogger(IT_TICKET_SCHEDULE_SCOPE)
        .warn(
          `IT ticket overdue reminder failed at startup: ${
            error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
          }`,
        );
    }
  }

E32 · app/server/services/it-ticket-service.ts:843–890

remindOverdue 只找 overdueNotifiedAt 为空的超时工单，先写标记再发送，失败回退标记为 null。

  async remindOverdue(): Promise<number> {
    const cutoff = new Date(Date.now() - OVERDUE_AFTER_MS);
    const repo = this.repository;
    const tickets = await repo.findMany({
      filter: (f) =>
        f.and([
          f.or([
            f.string('status').eq('pending'),
            f.string('status').eq('in_progress'),
          ]),
          f.date('createdAt').before(cutoff),
          f.date('overdueNotifiedAt').empty(),
        ]),
      sort: (s) => [s.field('createdAt').asc()],
      limit: 100,
    });
    if (!tickets.length) return 0;

    const serviceDeskIds = await this.userIdsFor('it-service-desk', true);
    if (!serviceDeskIds.length) return 0;

    let sent = 0;
    for (const ticket of tickets) {
      const now = new Date();
      const marker = await repo.updateOne({
        filter: (f) =>
          f.and([
            f.number('id').eq(ticket.id),
            f.date('overdueNotifiedAt').empty(),
          ]),
        values: { overdueNotifiedAt: now, updatedAt: now },
      });
      if (!marker.record) continue;

      const delivered = await this.options.notifier.overdue(
        { id: ticket.id, title: ticket.title },
        serviceDeskIds,
      );
      if (delivered) {
        sent += 1;
      } else {
        await repo.updateOne({
          filter: (f) => f.number('id').eq(ticket.id),
          values: { overdueNotifiedAt: null, updatedAt: new Date() },
        });
      }
    }
    return sent;

E36 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:124–126

Skill 要求 policy 覆盖每个动作，说明上传会继承 create scope/defaults 并替换字段白名单，同时明确内容路由公开且 Policy 不覆盖。

`policy` is required and governs every action of the exposure, uploads included. It is a Repository Policy: `read`, `create`, `update` and `delete`, each `true`, `false`, or a rule node. A node that is `false` refuses that operation; a node with no `fields` accepts no caller-supplied field, which is what a metadata `createOne` needs before it will accept anything. Pass a function of the principal — `policy: (principal) => ({ ... })` — together with a `principal(context)` resolver to scope rows to the caller; without a principal the request is refused with 403.

An upload supplies no caller fields, so it binds a Policy derived from this one: the `create` scope and defaults are inherited and the field allowlist is replaced by the file columns. `create: false` therefore forbids uploading as well as creating metadata — decided, with the principal, before the body limit, the content type or the multipart body is read, so a refused upload answers `403 WRITE_FORBIDDEN` (domain `app`) and stores nothing — and a `create.defaults` of `{ ownerId }` is stamped onto uploaded rows — which is what keeps an uploaded file inside the same scope `findMany` reads. The content route under `accessPath` is the exception: it is public and this Policy does not reach it.

原文件 SHA-256：55b6a892b607483ed34ac272db84d06a8d6e2bdd134747b7288021cff11ba480

E37 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-file/SKILL.md:130–130

Skill 要求受限文件在 /<name>/<action> 与 <accessPath>/* 前注册应用认证/授权，并检查 operation 与记录/父记录 access；登录页、私有磁盘或字段白名单不算认证。

These are public routes. For restricted files, register App-owned authentication and authorization on the paths each contribution owns before mounting it — `/<name>/<action>` per exposed API action, `<accessPath>/*` for content. Never `router.use('*', ...)` in a contribution router: contributions share the mounted router, so it also guards the SPA and every contribution mounted after yours. Check the operation and record/parent-record access; a login page, private disk, Client filter or Policy field allowlist is not authentication. Add scoped middleware and principal-based Policy to the generated routes first. If an existing contract needs an adapter, extend that module using the public Server manager and retain its authorization checks; missing built-in authentication alone is not a reason to replace the routes. Never trust browser-supplied ownership.

E38 · app/client/pages/it-tickets/screenshot-validation.ts:60–83

客户端按 PNG IHDR/IDAT/IEND 结构判断文件完整性，拒绝截断或缺少图像数据的文件。

function isCompletePng(bytes: Uint8Array): boolean {
  const signature = [0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47, 0x0d, 0x0a, 0x1a, 0x0a];
  if (!startsWith(bytes, signature)) return false;

  let offset = signature.length;
  let first = true;
  let imageData = false;
  while (offset + 12 <= bytes.length) {
    const length = readUint32BE(bytes, offset);
    const type = ascii(bytes, offset + 4, 4);
    const end = offset + 12 + length;
    if (end > bytes.length) return false;
    if (first) {
      if (type !== 'IHDR' || length !== 13) return false;
      first = false;
    }
    if (type === 'IDAT' && length > 0) imageData = true;
    if (type === 'IEND') {
      return length === 0 && end === bytes.length && imageData;
    }
    offset = end;
  }
  return false;
}

{"type":"tool_execution_update","toolCallId":"call_01_ET_leCubU32Ms8501NG4POC2754","toolName":"bash","args":{"command":"agent-browser skills get core 2>&1 | head -100","timeout":60},"partialResult":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"---\nname: core\ndescription: Core agent-browser usage guide. Read this before running any agent-browser commands. Covers the snapshot-and-ref workflow, navigating pages, interacting with elements (click, fill, type, select), extracting text and data, taking screenshots, managing tabs, handling forms and auth, waiting for content, running multiple browser sessions in parallel, and troubleshooting common failures. Use when the user asks to interact with a website, fill a form, click something, extract data, take a screenshot, log into a site, test a web app, or automate any browser task.\nallowed-tools: Bash(agent-browser:*), Bash(npx agent-browser:*)\n---\n\n# agent-browser core\n\nFast browser automation CLI for AI agents. Chrome/Chromium via CDP, no Playwright or Puppeteer dependency. Accessibility-tree snapshots with compact `@eN` refs let agents interact with pages in ~200-400 tokens instead of parsing raw HTML.\n\nMost normal web tasks (navigate, read, click, fill, extract, screenshot) are covered here. Load a specialized skill when the task falls outside browser web pages — see [When to load another skill](#when-to-load-another-skill).\n\n## The core loop\n\n```bash\nagent-browser open <url>        # 1. Open a page\nagent-browser snapshot -i       # 2. See what's on it (interactive elements only)\nagent-browser click @e3         # 3. Act on refs from the snapshot\nagent-browser snapshot -i       # 4. Re-snapshot after any page change\n```\n\nRefs (`@e1`, `@e2`, ...) are assigned fresh on every snapshot. They become **stale the moment the page changes** — after clicks that navigate, form submits, dynamic re-renders, dialog opens. Always re-snapshot before your next ref interaction.\n\n## Always use your own session\n\nBefore your first command, set a named session for the whole task:\n\n```bash\nexport AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION=\"$(agent-browser session id --scope worktree --prefix task)\"\n```\n\nThe default (unnamed) session is a single shared browser: it is shared with every other agent on the machine and it persists across conversations, so working in it can hijack another agent's page mid-task or navigate away from something the human left open. Every example below assumes a named session is active. See [Run multiple browsers in parallel](#run-multiple-browsers-in-parallel) and `references/session-management.md`.\n\n## Quickstart\n\n```bash\n# Install once\nnpm i -g agent-browser && agent-browser install\n\n# Linux hosts can install required browser libraries too\nagent-browser install --with-deps\n\n# Take a screenshot of a page\nagent-browser open https://example.com\nagent-browser screenshot home.png\nagent-browser close\n\n# Search, click a result, and capture it\nagent-browser open https://duckduckgo.com\nagent-browser snapshot -i                      # find the search box ref\nagent-browser fill @e1 \"agent-browser cli\"\nagent-browser press Enter\nagent-browser wait --load networkidle\nagent-browser snapshot -i                      # refs now reflect results\nagent-browser click @e5                        # click a result\nagent-browser screenshot result.png\n```\n\nThe browser stays running across commands so these feel like a single session. By default, an inactive daemon saves configured restore state, closes its headless browser, and exits after one hour; the next command starts it again. Without `--restore` or another restore key, shutdown discards transient browser state and open tabs. Dashboard mouse, keyboard, and touch input count as activity. Headed browsers, Safari and iOS WebDriver sessions, and user-attached browsers are exempt from the default; provider-owned cloud browsers are not. Use `--idle-timeout <time>` or `AGENT_BROWSER_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` to tune the timeout, and use `0` to disable it. Still run `agent-browser close` (or `close --all`) when you're done.\n\n## MCP integration\n\nFor tools that support Model Context Protocol servers, start the stdio server:\n\n```bash\nagent-browser mcp\nagent-browser mcp --tools all\nagent-browser mcp --tools core,network,react\n```\n\nConfigure the MCP client to launch `agent-browser` with `[\"mcp\"]`. The server defaults to MCP protocol 2025-11-25 and accepts older supported client protocol versions during initialization. The default tools profile is `core`, which keeps MCP context small for everyday browser automation. Use `--tools all` for the full typed CLI parity surface, or combine profiles with commas, such as `--tools core,network,react`. Profiles are `core`, `network`, `state`, `debug`, `tabs`, `react`, `mobile`, and `all`; the `debug` profile includes accessibility audits, plugin registry, and command.run tools. Each tool accepts typed arguments plus `extraArgs` for advanced CLI flags and exact CLI parity. The common `allowedDomains` array maps to `--allowed-domains` and activates the same WebRTC containment and launch-mode restrictions, while `idleTimeout` maps to `--idle-timeout`. Tool discovery is paginated and includes read-only/open-world annotations so modern MCP clients can load the large typed surface incrementally. Use the tool `session` argument or `AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION` to isolate browser sessions.\n\n## eve agent integration\n\nFor eve agents, mount the `@agent-browser/eve` extension instead of hand-writing browser tools. It adds namespaced tools such as `browser__navigate`, `browser__snapshot`, `browser__click`, `browser__fill`, `browser__find`, and `browser__screenshot`, all backed by agent-browser running inside the eve sandbox. The sandbox bootstrap helpers (`installAgentBrowser`, `agentBrowserRevalidationKey`) ship with the same package under `@agent-browser/eve/sandbox`, so `agent/sandbox.ts` needs no extra dependency.\n\n## Reading a page\n\n```bash\nagent-browser snapshot                    # full tree (verbose)\nagent-browser snapshot -i                 # interactive elements only (preferred)\nagent-browser snapshot -i -u              # include href urls on links\nagent-browser snapshot -i -c              # compact (no empty structural nodes)\nagent-browser snapshot -i -d 3            # cap depth at 3 levels\nagent-browser snapshot -s \"#main\"         # scope to a CSS selector\nagent-browser snapshot -i --json          # machine-readable output\n```\n\nSnapshot output looks like:\n\n```\nPage: Example - Log in\nURL: https://example.com/login\n\n@e1 [heading] \"Log in\"\n@e2 [form]\n  @e3 [input type=\"email\"] placeholder=\"Email\"\n  @e4 [input type=\"password\"] placeholder=\"Password\"\n  @e5 [button type=\"submit\"] \"Continue\"\n  @e6 [link] \"Forgot password?\"\n"}],"details":{}}}
